.

Given some internal vector
p^{I}
on
\mathcal{M}
, such a gauge element
\mathchoice{\Lambda^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\Lambda^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\Lambda^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\Lambda^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}
can always be found (unless there are topological obstructions). In other words, the homogenous curvature of the underlying spacetime metric
g_{ab}=\eta_{IJ}\mathchoice{e^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{e^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{e^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{e^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}\mathchoice{e^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}}}}{e^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}}}}{e^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}}}}{e^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}}}}
has been encoded into a
flat

\mathfrak{so}(1,2)
connection with non-vanishing
\mathfrak{an}_{2}
-valued torsion
T^{I}=\mathrm{d}_{\Omega}e^{I}
. This was a key feature that simplified the theory at the discrete level
Dupuis:2020ndx
, and this simplification will also be relevant for us to introduce a teleparallel equivalent of gravity in the case of homogeneously curved geometries that we will discuss below.

Before going to the general case, let us compare what we have just done with respect to
Dupuis:2020ndx
. There, the starting action is

\displaystyle{S}[e,A]

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\frac{1}{16\pi G}\bigg[\int_{\mathcal{M}}{\varepsilon_{IJK}\,e^{K}\wedge\Big(R^{IJ}[A]-\frac{\Lambda}{3}e^{I}\wedge e^{J}\Big)}-{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{\partial\mathcal{M}}\Big(\varepsilon_{IJK}r^{K}e^{I}\wedge e^{J}\Big)\bigg]

(47)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{16\pi G}\int_{\mathcal{M}}{\varepsilon_{IJK}\,e^{I}\wedge\bigg(R^{JK}[\Omega]\,+{\frac{1}{2}}\,e^{[J}\wedge\mathrm{d}_{\Omega}r^{K]}\bigg)},

but the internal Lorentz vector
r^{I}
is
not
interpreted as the normal of the boundary. In order to not increase the number of degrees of freedom, it is assumed that
\delta[r^{I}]=0
. This ensures that the
\mathfrak{so}(1,3)
connection
\mathchoice{A^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{A^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{A^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{A^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}
is shifted to
\tilde{\Omega}^{IJ}=A^{IJ}-\,r^{[I}e^{J]}
just as in (
28
). The vector
r^{I}
is also normalized as in (
32
) in order to cancel the volume term,

r_{I}r^{I}=-4\Lambda.

(48)

Without loss of generality, the vector
r
is assumed to have only one non-zero component, which is proportional to the square root of the (absolute value of) the cosmological constant. As a consequence,
\mathrm{d}r^{I}=0
and the equations of motion then coincide with the generalized curvature and torsion being zero.
We note that there is always a choice of gauge such that the normal
p^{I}
can be the constant vector
r^{I}
, so that the two approaches are the same.

III.2
Beyond three dimensions

In the previous section, we considered gravity in three dimensions. Our next step is to generalize the construction to arbitrary spacetime dimensions
d\geq 3
. Given the
\mathfrak{so}(1,d-1)
spin connection
\mathchoice{A^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{A^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{A^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{A^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}
, its conjugate momentum is the gravitational
B
-field, which is a bivector-valued
(d-2)
-form,

B_{IJ}[e]=\frac{1}{(d-2)!}\,\varepsilon_{IJK_{1}\cdots K_{d-2}}\,e^{K_{1}}\wedge\cdots\wedge e^{K_{d-2}},

(49)

where
e^{I}
is the co-frame that defines the metric
g_{ab}=\eta_{IJ}\mathchoice{e^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{e^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{e^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{e^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}\mathchoice{e^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}}}}{e^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}}}}{e^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}}}}{e^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}}}}
. Consider then the usual Einstein – Cartan action with a boundary term proportional to the volume of the boundary

\displaystyle S_{\text{EC}}[e,A,p]=\frac{1}{16\pi G}

\displaystyle\left[\int_{\mathcal{M}}\left(B_{IJ}[e]\wedge R^{IJ}[A]-\frac{2\Lambda}{d!}\varepsilon_{I_{1}\dots I_{d}}e^{I_{1}}\wedge\dots\wedge e^{I_{d}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\hskip 80.00012pt\left.-\frac{1}{(d-1)!}\int_{\partial\mathcal{M}}p^{J}\varepsilon_{JI_{1}\dots I_{d-1}}e^{I_{1}}\wedge\dots\wedge e^{I_{d-1}}\right],

(50)

where
R^{IJ}[A]
is the
\mathfrak{so}(1,d-1)
-valued curvature two-form
\mathchoice{R^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{R^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{R^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{R^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}[A]=\mathrm{d}\mathchoice{A^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{A^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884 , and
p_{I}
is an internal vector (a vector-valued
0
-form). The critical points of the action are found by imposing the following boundary conditions,

\mathchoice{h^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}}{h^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}}{h^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}}{h^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}}\mathchoice{h^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}}{h^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scri p_{I}\varphi^{\ast}_{\partial\mathcal{M}}e^{I}=0,

(51)

where
\varphi^{\ast}_{\partial\mathcal{M}}:T^{\ast}\mathcal{M}\rightarrow T^{\ast}(\partial\mathcal{M})
is the pull-back and
h_{IJ}=sn_{I}n_{J}+h_{IJ}
is the projector onto the boundary such that
n_{I}n^{I}=s\in\{\pm 1\}
, and
\mathchoice{h^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{h^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{h^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{h^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}\varphi^{\ast}_{\partial\mathcal{M}}e^{J}=\varphi^{\ast}_{\partial\mathcal{M}}e^{I}
. The resulting field equations are the
d
-dimensional Einstein equations for the metric
g_{ab}=e_{Ia}\mathchoice{e^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}}}}{e^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}}}}{e^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}}}}{e^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[3.51666pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.1029pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.50208pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{b}}}}
with a cosmological constant
\Lambda
.

Consider then a region
\Sigma
within the boundary, i.e.
\Sigma\subset\partial\mathcal{M}
. The pre-symplectic potential is obtained from the first variation of the action (
50
). Taking into account the boundary conditions (
51
), we obtain

\Theta_{\Sigma}(\delta)=\frac{(-1)^{(d-2)}}{16\pi G}\int_{\Sigma}B_{IJ}\wedge\delta\Big[A^{IJ}-p^{[I}e^{J]}\Big]\equiv\frac{(-1)^{(d-2)}}{16\pi G}\int_{\Sigma}B_{IJ}\wedge\delta[\Omega^{IJ}].

(52)

Next, we need to express the field equations in terms of the new and shifted connection
\Omega
. This requires smoothly extending the internal boundary vector
p_{I}
into the bulk such that
p_{I}\varphi^{\ast}_{\partial\mathcal{M}}e^{I}=0
is still satisfied. Given such an extension of
p_{I}
from the boundary into the bulk, we introduce the shifted connection

\displaystyle\Omega^{IJ}

\displaystyle=A^{IJ}+e^{[I}p^{J]}\equiv A^{IJ}-{\cal I}^{IJ},

(53)

\displaystyle{\cal I}^{IJ}

\displaystyle\equiv p^{[I}e^{J]}={\frac{1}{2}}C^{IJ}{}_{K}e^{K},\quad C^{IJ}{}_{K}=(p^{I}\delta^{J}_{K}-p^{J}\delta^{I}_{K}).

(54)

Just as in three-dimensions, we reformulate the action in terms of the new connection. Consider first the curvature scalar,

\displaystyle B_{IJ}[e]\wedge R^{IJ}[A]

\displaystyle=B_{IJ}[e]\wedge\left(R^{IJ}[{{\Omega}}]+\mathrm{d}_{\Omega}{\cal I}^{IJ}+\mathchoice{{\cal I}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}}{{\cal I}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}}{{\cal I}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}}{{\cal I}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}}\wedge{{\cal I}}^{KJ}\right)

\displaystyle=B_{IJ}[e]\wedge\left(R^{IJ}[{{\Omega}}]+\mathrm{d}_{\Omega}{\cal I}^{IJ}+\frac{1}{2}p^{[I}p\wedge e^{J]}-\frac{p_{K}p^{K}}{4}e^{I}\wedge e^{J}\right),

(55)

where
p
denotes the one-form
p=p_{I}e^{I}
. We then also have the identity

p_{M}p^{M}\frac{1}{d!}\varepsilon_{I_{1}\dots I_{d}}e^{I_{1}}\wedge\dots\wedge e^{I_{d}}=\frac{1}{(d-1)!}p^{J}\varepsilon_{JI_{1}\dots I_{d-1}}p\wedge e^{I_{1}}\dots e^{I_{d-1}}.

(56)

We thus have

\frac{1}{2}B_{IJ}\wedge\left(p^{I}p\wedge e^{J}-\frac{p_{K}p^{K}}{2}e^{I}\wedge e^{J}\right)=-\frac{p_{M}p^{M}}{4}\frac{(d-1)(d-2)}{d!}\varepsilon_{I_{1}\dots I_{d}}e^{I_{1}}\wedge\dots\wedge e^{I_{d}}.

(57)

If we impose the mass shell condition

p_{I}p^{I}=-\frac{8\Lambda}{(d-1)(d-2)},

(58)

then the action (
50
) simplifies

\displaystyle S_{\text{EC}}[e,\Omega,p]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{16\pi G}\left[\int_{\mathcal{M}}B_{IJ}\wedge\Big(R^{IJ}[\Omega]+\mathrm{d}_{\Omega}{\cal I}^{IJ}\Big)-\frac{(-1)^{(d-2)}}{d-1}\int_{\partial\mathcal{M}}B_{IJ}\wedge p^{I}e^{J}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{16\pi G}\int_{\mathcal{M}}\left[B_{IJ}\wedge R^{IJ}[\Omega]+B_{IJ}\wedge\left(\mathrm{d}_{\Omega}{\cal I}^{IJ}-p^{[I}\mathrm{d}_{\Omega}e^{J]}-\frac{1}{d-1}(\mathrm{d}_{\Omega}p^{[I})\wedge e^{J]}\right)\right].

(59)

Taking into account that
\mathcal{I}^{IJ}=p^{[I}e^{J]}
, one finally arrives at the expression

S_{\text{EC}}[e,\Omega,p]=\frac{1}{16\pi G}\int_{\mathcal{M}}\Big(B_{IJ}\wedge R^{IJ}[\Omega]-(d-2)(-1)^{d-2}B_{I}\wedge\mathrm{d}_{\Omega}p^{I}\Big),

(60)

where we introduced the vector-valued
(d-1)
-form,

B_{I}=\frac{1}{(d-1)!}\varepsilon_{IK_{1}\dots K_{d-1}}e^{K_{1}}\wedge\dots\wedge e^{K_{d-1}}.

(61)

The Einstein equations are the saddle points of the action (
60
) in the space of all fields that satisfy the mass shell constraint (
58
) and boundary conditions

\mathchoice{h^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}}{h^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}}{h^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}}{h^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}}\mathchoice{h^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}}{h^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}}{h^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}}{h^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}}\varphi^{\ast}_{\partial\mathcal{M}}\delta[\Omega^{KL}]=0,\qquad\varphi^{\ast}_{\partial\mathcal{M}}(p_{I}e^{I})=0.

(62)

The variation of the frame field for boundary conditions (
62
) yields the curvature constraint

\frac{1}{(d-3)!}\varepsilon_{IJKL_{1}\dots L_{d-3}}e^{L_{1}}\wedge\dots\wedge e^{L_{d-3}}\wedge\left(\underbrace{R^{JK}[\Omega]+e^{[J}\wedge\mathrm{d}_{\Omega}p^{K]}}_{\mathcal{F}^{JK}}\right)=0.

(63)

By varying the action (
60
) with respect to
\Omega
and taking into account the boundary conditions (
62
), we find that the deformed torsion vanishes
4
4

4

Provided
e^{I_{1}}\wedge\dots\wedge e^{I_{d}}\neq 0
.

\mathcal{T}^{I}=\mathrm{d}_{\Omega}e^{I}+\frac{1}{4}\,\mathchoice{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.72687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.0906pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{IJ}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.72687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.0906pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{IJ}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.72687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.0906pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{IJ}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.72687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.0906pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{IJ}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}\,e^{I}\wedge e^{J}=0,

(64)

where the structure constants
\mathchoice{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.72687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.0906pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{IJ}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.72687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.0906pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{IJ}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.72687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.0906pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{IJ}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[9.17293pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[5.72687pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.0906pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{K}}}_{{{IJ}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}
are given in (
54
). Finally, we should also consider the variations of
p^{I}
. Taking into account the mass shell condition (
58
), we obtain

\mathrm{d}_{\Omega}B_{I}\propto p_{I}.

(65)

This condition is satisfied once we solve for (
64
). Indeed, (
64
) implies

\mathrm{d}_{\Omega}B_{I}=\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{(d-2)!}\varepsilon_{IK_{1}\dots K_{d-1}}p\wedge e^{K_{1}}\wedge\dots e^{K_{d-1}}=\frac{d-1}{2}p_{I}\,d^{d}v.

(66)

As in three dimensions, the new variables deform the torsion two-form
T^{I}=\mathrm{d}_{\Omega}e^{I}
, which is now sourced by the cosmological constant. In addition, we also see that a flat
\mathfrak{so}(1,d)
connection
\mathchoice{\Omega^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\Omega^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\Omega^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\Omega^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}
solves the curvature equation (
63
) provided
\mathrm{d}_{\Omega}p^{I}=0
. This observation will be relevant once we consider a lattice approach, where the field equations are solved by imposing that the connection is piecewise flat.

The nature of the boundary (spacelike, timelike, or null) depends via the mass shell condition (
58
) on the sign of
\Lambda
. The following table summarizes the situation for both Euclidean and Lorentzian signature in arbitrary dimensions.

IV
Metric and deformed teleparallel formulation in the new variables

IV.1
Recovering unimodular gravity

Generalized covariant derivative.

The Levi-Civita connection defines the unique torsionless covariant derivative that is metric compatible. In the last section, we deformed the notion of torsion. Let us now introduce the corresponding covariant derivative on the tangent bundle, i.e. the corresponding metric formulation.

For any given and invertible
d
-bein
\mathchoice{e^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{e^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{e^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}{e^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{a}}}}
and given Lorentz vector
p^{I}
, the field equation

\mathrm{d}_{\Omega}e^{I}+\frac{1}{4}\,C_{JK}{}^{I}\,e^{J}\wedge e^{K}=T^{I}+\frac{1}{4}\,C_{JK}{}^{I}\,e^{J}\wedge e^{K}=0

(67)

has a unique solution

The corresponding metric compatible covariant derivative is defined for any smooth vector field

via

(70)

This definition generalizes naturally to arbitrary tensor fields. For any two such vector fields, we may then define curvature and torsion of this new connection,

\displaystyle={\accentset{\circ}{\nabla}}_{a}{\accentset{\circ}{\nabla}}_{b}V^{c}-{\accentset{\circ}{\nabla}}_{b}{\accentset{\circ}{\nabla}}_{a}V^{c}-\accentset{\circ}{T}\mathchoice{\vphantom{T}{}^{{{d}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{\vphantom{T}{}^{{{d}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{\vphantom{T}{}^{{{d}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{\vphantom{T}{}^{{{d}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.16287pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.55038pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.8217pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{\accentset{\circ}{\nabla}}_{d}V^{c},

(71)

\displaystyle\accentset{\circ}{T}\mathchoice{\vphantom{T}{}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{bc}}}}{\vphantom{T}{}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{bc}}}}{\vphantom{T}{}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{bc}}}}{\vphantom{T}{}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{bc}}}}U^{b}V^{c}

\displaystyle=U^{b}{\accentset{\circ}{\nabla}}_{b}V^{a}-V^{b}{\accentset{\circ}{\nabla}}_{b}U^{a}-[U,V]^{a}.

C_{abc}=2p_{[a}g_{b]c},

(73)

which is, of course, the same as (
67
), but now written in the standard tensor language. Note that we can either interpret the Christoffel symbol
\accentset{\circ}{\Gamma}{}^{a}{}_{bc}={\Gamma}{}^{a}{}_{bc}+C{}^{a}{}_{bc}
as having torsion in the usual sense, or it has vanishing generalized torsion
\mathchoice{{\cal T}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{{\cal T}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{{\cal T}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}{{\cal T}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.85431pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.693pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.35213pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{ab}}}}=0
.

Recovering unimodular gravity.

As emphasized for example in
BeltranJimenez:2019tjy
, the gravitational force can be encoded into various geometric entities, namely an affine connection
\mathchoice{\tilde{\Gamma}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{bc}}}}{\tilde{\Gamma}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{bc}}}}{\tilde{\Gamma}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{bc}}}}{\tilde{\Gamma}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{bc}}}}
, non-metricity
\tilde{Q}_{abc}
, or torsion
\mathchoice{\tilde{T}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{bc}}}}{\tilde{T}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{bc}}}}{\tilde{T}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{bc}}}}{\tilde{T}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.09041pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.2234pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.01671pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{bc}}}}
. Our choice of variables provides a connection
\accentset{\circ}{\Gamma}{}^{a}{}_{bc}
where
\accentset{\circ}{Q}{}^{a}{}_{bc}=0
but
\accentset{\circ}{T}{}^{a}{}_{bc}\neq 0
. The corresponding
second-order action
is obtained by going
half-shell
, i.e. by reinserting the solution for the connection
\accentset{\circ}{\Omega}{}^{I}{}_{Ja}
in terms of
p_{I}
and
e_{Ia}
back into the
first-order
action (
59
). A short calculation gives

S_{\text{EH}}[g,p]:=S_{\text{EC}}\Big[e,\accentset{\circ}{\Omega}[e,p],p\Big]=\frac{1}{16\pi G}\int_{\mathcal{M}}d^{d}v_{g}\Big(g^{cb}\accentset{\circ}{R}\mathchoice{\vphantom{R}{}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.81349pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.86676pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{cab}}}}{\vphantom{R}{}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.81349pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.86676pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{cab}}}}{\vphantom{R}{}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.81349pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.86676pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{cab}}}}{\vphantom{R}{}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.81349pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.86676pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{cab}}}}[g,p]+(d-2)\overset{\circ}{\nabla}_{a}p^{a}\Big),

(74)

where
d^{d}v_{g}
is the usual metrical volume element.
5
5

5

The volume element
d^{d}v_{g}
is the
d
-form, whose components are the Levi-Civita tensor, i.e.
(d^{d}v_{g})_{abc\dots}=\varepsilon_{abc\dots}
.
This action resembles the Henneaux-Teitelboim model for unimodular gravity
Henneaux:1989zc
. In fact, to obtain the Einstein equations, this action is to be extremized in the space of all fields
(g_{ab},p^{c})
that satisfy the mass shell condition (
58
) that now simply reads

g_{ab}p^{a}p^{b}=-\frac{8\Lambda}{(d-1)(d-2)}.

(75)

It seems now natural to relax this condition, namely by adding a Lagrange multiplier that will impose the mass-shell constraint at the dynamical level. At the same time, this also allows us to unfreeze
\Lambda
, obtaining a version of unimodular gravity. Consider, in fact, a
(d-1)
-form
\tau
. Its exterior derivative defines a volume density
\tilde{N}=\mathrm{d}\tau
. Varying the action

S_{\text{\it unimod}}[g,p,{\tau}]=\frac{1}{16\pi G}\int_{\mathcal{M}}\left[d^{d}v_{g}\Big(g^{cb}\accentset{\circ}{R}\mathchoice{\vphantom{R}{}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.81349pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.86676pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{cab}}}}{\vphantom{R}{}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.81349pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.86676pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{cab}}}}{\vphantom{R}{}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.81349pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.86676pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{cab}}}}{\vphantom{R}{}^{{{a}\mathchoice{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[11.42805pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[6.81349pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[4.86676pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.33765pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.59009pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.85005pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{cab}}}}[g,p]+(d-2)\overset{\circ}{\nabla}_{a}p^{a}\Big)-\frac{1}{2}g_{ab}p^{a}p^{b}\,\mathrm{d}\tau\right]

(76)

with respect to
\tau
, will tell us then that
g_{ab}p^{a}p^{b}
is constant for
some

\Lambda
, while all other variations return the usual Einstein equations with respect to the deformed connection
\accentset{\circ}{\nabla}_{a}
. In this way,
\tau
provides a notion of time,
p^{a}
plays the role of a space-time momentum, and
\sqrt{|\Lambda|}
represent the rest mass of
p^{a}
.

IV.2
Deformed teleparallel gravity

The new variables deform curvature and torsion, see (
39
) and (
40
). The gravity action can be encoded in the curvature when there is no torsion or vice-versa in the torsion if there is no curvature. The latter viewpoint underlies the teleparallel framework. We want to see now how the new variables provide a non-Abelian version of teleparallelism, where the translational connection takes values in
\mathfrak{an}_{d-1}
, whereas the Lorentzian part of the
\mathfrak{so}(1,d-1)\bowtie\mathfrak{an}_{d-1}
connection is flat.

Consider a generic
\mathfrak{so}(1,d-1)\bowtie\mathfrak{an}_{d-1}
connection
\boldsymbol{A}
. The space of such
\mathfrak{so}(1,d)\bowtie\mathfrak{an}_{d-1}
connections is an affine space. Since it is an affine space, any such connection can be written as a sum of some arbitrary reference connection
\accentset{\bullet}{\boldsymbol{A}}
and a difference tensor
\boldsymbol{\Delta}
,

\boldsymbol{A}=\accentset{\bullet}{\boldsymbol{A}}-\boldsymbol{\Delta}.

(77)

With this decomposition, we have

\boldsymbol{F}[\boldsymbol{A}]=\accentset{\bullet}{\boldsymbol{F}}-\accentset{\bullet}{\boldsymbol{D}}\boldsymbol{\Delta}+\frac{1}{2}\left[\boldsymbol{\Delta}\wedge\boldsymbol{\Delta}\right],

(78)

where
\accentset{\bullet}{\boldsymbol{F}}
is the curvature of
\accentset{\bullet}{\boldsymbol{A}}
and
\accentset{\bullet}{\boldsymbol{D}}(\cdot)=\boldsymbol{\mathrm{d}}(\cdot)+[\accentset{\bullet}{\boldsymbol{A}},\cdot]
is the covariant derivative as in (
45
), (
42
) above.

The deformed
\mathfrak{an}_{d-1}
Lie-algebra valued equivalent of teleparallel gravity corresponds to the following choice of reference connection,

\accentset{\bullet}{\boldsymbol{A}}=\frac{1}{2}\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{IJ}\otimes J_{IJ}+e^{I}\otimes P_{I},

(79)

where
J_{IJ}
and
P_{I}
are the generators of
\mathfrak{so}(1,d-1)\bowtie\mathfrak{an}_{d-1}
with commutation relations (
43a
,
43b
,
43c
). Notice that
\boldsymbol{A}
and
\accentset{\bullet}{\boldsymbol{A}}
have the same projection onto
\mathfrak{an}_{d}
. Therefore,
\boldsymbol{\Delta}
is a pure rotation,

\boldsymbol{\Delta}=\frac{1}{2}\Delta^{IJ}\otimes J_{IJ}.

(80)

We are now left to specify the reference connection
\mathchoice{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}
, and we choose it to satisfy the following two conditions:

\displaystyle\mathchoice{R^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{R^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{R^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{R^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}[\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}]

\displaystyle=\mathrm{d}\mathchoice{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}+\mathchoice{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$ ptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{{{K}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[7.20976pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[4.51207pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[3.2229pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}=0,

(81)

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}_{\Omega}p_{I}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{d}p_{I}-\mathchoice{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}}{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}}{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}}{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{{{J}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}}p_{J}=0.

(82)

Notice that there always exists a Lorentz transformation
\Lambda{}^{I}{}_{J}
such that
p_{I}=\Lambda{}^{J}{}_{I}\,r_{J}
, where
r_{J}
is a constant vector, i.e.
\mathrm{d}r_{J}=0
. We can then choose

\mathchoice{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.08472pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.1884pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.27742pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{J}}}}=(\Lambda^{-1}\mathrm{d}\Lambda){}^{I}{}_{J}.

(83)

which will then solve both (
81
) and (
82
).
Therefore, the curvature of the
\mathfrak{so}(1,d-1)\bowtie\mathfrak{an}_{d-1}
connection has only a
\mathfrak{an}_{d-1}
part,

\accentset{\bullet}{\boldsymbol{F}}=\frac{1}{2}\accentset{\bullet}{\mathcal{F}}^{IJ}\otimes J_{IJ}+\accentset{\bullet}{\mathcal{T}}^{I}\otimes P_{I}=\accentset{\bullet}{\mathcal{T}}^{I}\otimes P_{I},

(84)

where we defined the
\mathfrak{an}_{d}
Lie algebra-valued torsion two-form

\accentset{\bullet}{\mathcal{T}}^{I}=\mathrm{d}\raisebox{1.99997pt}{${}_{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}}$}e^{I}+\frac{1}{4}\mathchoice{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}e^{J}\wedge e^{K}\equiv\accentset{\bullet}{\nabla}e^{I}+\frac{1}{4}\mathchoice{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{C^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{I}}}_{{{JK}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}e^{J}\wedge e^{K}.

(85)

If the field equation (
64
) is satisfied, i.e. by going
half-shell
, we can express the components of the difference tensor
\Delta_{IJ}=\Delta_{IJK}e^{K}
in terms of the components of the deformed
\mathfrak{an}_{d}
Lie algebra-valued torsion,

\Delta_{IJK}=-\frac{1}{2}\left(\accentset{\bullet}{\mathcal{T}}_{IJK}+\accentset{\bullet}{\mathcal{T}}_{JKI}-\accentset{\bullet}{\mathcal{T}}_{KIJ}\right).

(86)

Inserting this solution back into the first-order action (
60
), we obtain

\displaystyle S_{\text{EC}}\Big[e,\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}+\Delta,p\Big]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{16\pi G}\int_{\mathcal{M}}\bigg[B_{IJ}\wedge R^{IJ}[\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}]-(-1)^{d-3}B_{IJK}\wedge(\mathrm{d}\raisebox{1.99997pt}{${}_{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}}$}e^{K})\wedge\Delta^{IJ}-B_{IJ}\wedge\mathchoice{\Delta^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}}{\Delta^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}}{\Delta^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}}{\Delta^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}}\wedge\Delta^{LJ}

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt-(d-2)(-1)^{d-2}B_{I}\wedge\mathrm{d}\raisebox{1.99997pt}{${}_{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}}$}p^{I}+(d-2)(-1)^{d-2}B_{I}p_{J}\wedge\Delta^{IJ}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt-\frac{1}{16\pi G}\int_{\partial\mathcal{M}}(-1)^{d-2}B_{IJ}\wedge\Delta^{IJ}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{16\pi G}\int_{\mathcal{M}}\bigg[B_{IJ}\wedge\mathcal{F}^{IJ}[\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}]-(-1)^{d-3}B_{IJK}\wedge\accentset{\bullet}{\mathcal{T}}^{K}\wedge\Delta^{IJ}-B_{IJ}\wedge\mathchoice{\Delta^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}}{\Delta^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}}{\Delta^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}}{\Delta^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.48615pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.33472pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.38194pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{L}}}}\wedge\Delta^{LJ}

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt-B_{IJ}\wedge e^{I}\wedge\mathrm{d}\raisebox{1.99997pt}{${}_{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}}$}p^{J}+\frac{1}{2}(-1)^{d-3}B_{IJK}\wedge p\wedge e^{K}\wedge\Delta^{IJ}

(87)

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt-(d-2)(-1)^{d-2}B_{I}\wedge\mathrm{d}\raisebox{1.99997pt}{${}_{\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}}$}p^{I}+(d-2)(-1)^{d-2}B_{I}p_{J}\wedge\Delta^{IJ}\bigg]

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt-\frac{1}{16\pi G}\int_{\partial\mathcal{M}}(-1)^{d-2}B_{IJ}\wedge\Delta^{IJ},

(88)

where we performed a partial integration and used the definition of
\accentset{\bullet}{\boldsymbol{F}}=\frac{1}{2}\accentset{\bullet}{\mathcal{F}}^{IJ}\otimes J_{IJ}+\accentset{\bullet}{\mathcal{T}}^{I}\otimes P_{I}
for a generic
\mathfrak{so}(1,d-1)\bowtie\mathfrak{an}_{d-1}
connection
\accentset{\bullet}{\boldsymbol{A}}
, see also (
39
) and (
40
). In addition,

B_{I_{1}\dots I_{n}}:=\frac{1}{(d-n)!}\varepsilon_{I_{1}\dots I_{n}K_{1}\dots K_{d-n}}e^{K_{1}}\wedge\dots\wedge e^{K_{d-n}}.

(89)

If we then also use the algebraic identity

\frac{1}{n!}B_{I_{1}\dots I_{n}}\wedge e^{J_{1}}\wedge\dots e^{J_{n}}=(-1)^{n(d-n)}d^{d}v\,\delta^{[J_{1}}_{I_{1}}\dots\delta^{J_{n}]}_{I_{n}},

(90)

then we can write the action (
88
) in terms of contractions of
\mathchoice{\accentset{\bullet}{\mathcal{T}}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{KJ}}}}{\accentset{\bullet}{\mathcal{T}}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{KJ}}}}{\accentset{\bullet}{\mathcal{T}}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{KJ}}}}{\accentset{\bullet}{\mathcal{T}}^{{{I}\mathchoice{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[12.29448pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[7.70047pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[5.50032pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.08821pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.53847pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.81319pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{KJ}}}}
alone. A straight-forward calculation gives

\displaystyle S_{\text{EC}}\Big[e,\accentset{\bullet}{\Omega}+\Delta,p\Big]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{16\pi
